# 引擎代码审查补充发现（2026-06）

> 本文是 2026-06-22 一轮聚焦代码审查的发现目录，作为结构规范与各子计划的**补充输入**。每条带 file:line 证据、严重度与目标计划。规范级规则已并入 [`engine-code-structure-convention.md`](engine-code-structure-convention.md)；结构级发现已并入 Runtime 15 / Editor UI 10 / Plugins 12；属既有受 guard 计划（02/04/06/07/08/09、render）的项在此登记为"建议补入"，由该计划 owner 在其切片内吸收（避免直接改动受计数 guard 的文件）。
>
> 审查为只读，未改动代码；所有证据已实地核对，引用时仍须按计划纪律重核 live worktree。

## 1. P0 —— 健壮性 / 已知性能（优先排期）

| # | 发现 | 证据 | 建议 | 目标计划 |
|---|------|------|------|---------|
| F1 | **原生插件入站 host 回调必须在跨 FFI 前截断 panic**；原始审查发现 `host_api_adapter.rs` / `host_callbacks.rs` 的 native host 回调缺少 `catch_unwind`，panic 跨 FFI 会触发 UB | 现状已由 `plugin/native_plugin_loader/ffi_panic_guard.rs` 闭合：`host_api_adapter.rs` 9 个 public `ZrHostApiV3` callbacks 全部进入 `catch_native_host_api_panic` 并把 panic 映射为 `ZrStatusCode::Panic`；`host_callbacks.rs` 4 个 private native host callbacks 全部进入 `catch_native_plugin_host_callback_panic` 并映射为 `ZIRCON_NATIVE_PLUGIN_STATUS_PANIC`；`review_f1_native_host_callbacks_catch_unwind_before_crossing_ffi` 与 `p0_f1_f2_f4_top_row_closed_status_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 锁定本状态 | 保持所有交给插件的 `extern "C"` 回调统一套 panic guard；Runtime 15 状态 `runtime_15_native_host_callback_panic_guard_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 作为当前闭合锚，Cargo 按切片节奏 deferred | Runtime 15 + Runtime 06 + Plugins 11 / review closed |
| F2 | **scene/EventBus shared locks recover after poison**；原始审查发现 scene 每帧 world-state holder 与 EventBus prune 路径直接 `.lock().unwrap()`，mutex poison 会导致后续 runtime 路径崩溃 | 现状已由 `scene/level_system.rs` 的 `lock_poison_recovered(...)` 与 field-specific accessors、`scene/module/default_level_manager.rs::lock_levels()`、`core/runtime/events.rs::lock_subscribers()` / `lock_delivery()` 闭合；`level_system_accessors_recover_poisoned_state_locks`、`runtime_15_f2_lock_poison_recovery_guard_covers_scene_and_eventbus`、`review_f2_scene_eventbus_locks_recover_after_poison` 与 `p0_f1_f2_f4_top_row_closed_status_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 锁定 scene/EventBus poison-safe lock recovery complete | 保持 production 路径只通过 helper 恢复 poison，不回退 direct `.lock().unwrap()`；Runtime 15 状态 `runtime_15_f2_lock_poison_recovery_guard_core_min_cargo_passed_full_sweep_pending` 作为当前闭合锚，完整 Runtime 15 sweep 仍 pending | Runtime 15 + Runtime 07 / review closed |
| F3 | **每帧渲染提交整帧 `extract.clone()` ×3 + 相机循环内按相机二次整帧 clone + 几何/5 类光源 Vec 全拷贝**（吻合 ~10fps） | 现状已分段收敛：`FrameSubmissionContext` 使用 `source_extract: Arc<RenderFrameExtract>`，build-context 不再复制 mesh/5 类 light/previous-particle Vec，generated/direct camera loop 已改成 shared/streaming source，feedback/prepared sidebands 改 owned drain；2026-06-27 以 `resolve_camera_sequence_borrowed(&extract.view.cameras)` 去掉生产 `camera_loop_submissions()` 的 `extract.view.cameras.clone()` 序列预拷贝，把 generated/direct camera-loop 的 post-process source restore 收窄为 `CameraLoopPostProcessSourceState` 字段级快照；2026-06-28 先用 `restore_optional_payload_source(...)` 关闭 VG/HGI 每 child 无条件 restore clone，再让 single-child camera-loop 跳过 `CameraLoopExtractSourceState` / `CameraLoopFrameSourceState` 捕获与首 child restore，随后以 `FrameSubmissionSourcePayloads` + source payload slot ownership 关闭 multi-child source-state 的一次性 VG/HGI backup clone | 继续按 Runtime 07 关闭真实 FPS/profiling/full Runtime 07 gate；当前锚 `render_camera_loop_source_payload_slot_owned_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 只关闭 source-state payload slot clone，不声明 FPS/profiling 已达标 | Runtime 07 + render index |
| F4 | **render submit viewport/provider capability gaps return typed errors**；原始审查发现提交路径 viewport/VG `expect`/`unwrap` 无降级，provider 缺席或时序竞态会 panic 渲染线程 | 现状已由 `submit_frame_extract/viewport_generation_guard.rs` 的 `validate_viewport_generation(...)` / `viewport_record_mut_after_generation_check(...)`、三条 submit/present lane 的 checked record writeback，以及 `prepare_runtime_submission/prepare.rs::missing_runtime_provider(...)` 闭合；viewport 删除返回 `RenderFrameworkError::UnknownViewport`，generation 变化返回 `RenderFrameworkError::ViewportChanged`，HGI/VG provider 缺席返回 `RenderFrameworkError::UnsupportedCapability`；`review_f4_render_submit_capability_gaps_return_typed_errors` 与 `p0_f1_f2_f4_top_row_closed_status_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 锁定 production submit/prepare 段无 `.unwrap(`/`.expect(` 回流 | 保持 submit_frame_extract production paths must return RenderFrameworkError；Runtime 07 状态 `render_submit_viewport_provider_errors_review_guard_static_passed_cargo_timeout_no_result_full_runtime07_pending` 作为当前闭合锚，完整 Runtime 07 FPS/profiling/full gate 仍 pending | Runtime 07 + render index / review closed |

## 4. 正面基线（建议作为 PASS 基线登记，防回退）

- **契约 crate `zircon_runtime_interface` 边界干净**：依赖仅 glam/serde/serde_json/thiserror/toml/unicode-segmentation/uuid；无 wgpu/slint/winit/tokio；`#[repr(C)]` 无 `Vec`/`String`/`Box<dyn>`。证据：`zircon_runtime_interface/Cargo.toml:8-15`、`src/tests/boundary.rs:23-25`。→ Runtime 10 / runtime index 作 PASS 基线。
- **生产代码无 `TODO`/`FIXME`/`HACK` 注释**（zircon_runtime/src、zircon_editor/src 零命中）——债务藏在结构里而非注释，印证本目录"结构债优先"取向。
- **`ui/v2` 的 `expect` 多为带说明的合理不变式**（`file_cache.rs:408`、`loader.rs:81`、`style.rs:103`），非应返 Result 的误用。
- **editor/app 直接依赖 winit + softbuffer 是有意设计**（拥有原生窗口/事件循环），无 wgpu/slint 泄漏；建议规范明确"editor/app 允许 winit+softbuffer、禁 wgpu/slint"消除"authoring-only"标签歧义。证据：`zircon_editor/Cargo.toml:19,23`、`zircon_app/Cargo.toml:85-86`。

## 5. 优先级建议

当前最高优先剩余项按现状锚而非原始发现排序：P0 安全/崩溃类 **F1/F2/F4 已有闭合守卫**（`review_f1_native_host_callbacks_catch_unwind_before_crossing_ffi`、`review_f2_scene_eventbus_locks_recover_after_poison`、`review_f4_render_submit_capability_gaps_return_typed_errors`），继续保持防回退即可；性能侧 **F3 仍需 Runtime 07 full FPS/profiling/full gate**，当前最新锚 `render_camera_loop_source_payload_slot_owned_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 只关闭 source payload slot clone，不声明真实 FPS/profiling 完成；DX 侧 **D-S7 静态插件 manifest 生成/parity 已由 `ds7_static_plugin_manifest_generation_parity_review_synced_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 闭合**，当前 `tools/audit_plugin_structure.py --json` 为 36/37 generated manifest（native fixture 唯一手写），并由 `plugins_12_static_plugin_manifest_is_generated`、`plugins_12_manifest_schema_uniform_audit_report_is_clean`、`plugins_12_feature_enabled_runtime_descriptor_manifest_parity` 锁定；**native_dynamic_fixture importer 自描述已闭合**、**D-S8/D3 native fixture SDK macro/manifest 单源已闭合**、**D8 runtime registration builder 原始证据路径已由 `d8_runtime_registration_builder_original_paths_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 闭合**、**D13 runtime export macro/selection helper 已由 `d13_importer_runtime_export_macro_convergence_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 闭合**、**D13 importer runtime manifest builder 已由 `d13_importer_runtime_manifest_builder_convergence_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 闭合**、**D13 importer manifest parity guard 已由 `d13_importer_manifest_parity_guard_static_passed_cargo_deferred` 闭合**（`review_d13_importer_manifest_parity_guard_lives_in_sdk_builder` 锁定 SDK manifest parity 单测 `importer_runtime_manifest_builder_keeps_targets_platforms_modules_and_distribution_in_parity`）。`review_priority_recommendation_tracks_current_remaining_work` 锁定本段不回退到原始未闭合描述。

## 2026-06-28 Plugins 13 plugin validate preflight 审查补记

| 日期 | 范围 | 状态 | 审查发现处理记录 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| 2026-06-28 | Editor UI 11 M5 structure convention descriptor family guard | plan_11_structure_convention_descriptor_family_guard_passed | 审查发现：结构规范 §5 R5.3 仍把 `.zui` / `.ui.toml` / `.zmaterial` 写成同一活动 descriptor family 串，容易让已退役 `.ui.toml` / `.v2.ui.toml` 被误当作当前 UI asset schema owner 回流；同时 layout metadata `.toml` 需要保留 owner 身份，不能被混同为 UI asset descriptor。本轮新增 docs guard 锁定 R5.3：`.zui` 是唯一 UI asset descriptor 家族，`.ui.toml` / `.v2.ui.toml` 已退役，`page_templates.toml`、`shell_regions.toml` 与 `presets.toml` 只作为 typed editor layout metadata 索引 `.zui` UI 资产。该切片未改 runtime/editor 生产代码，未新增兼容层或 Cargo 结论。 |

## 2026-06-28 Plan 08 shader resource registry-backed locator correlation 审查补记

| 日期 | 范围 | 状态 | 审查发现处理记录 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-06-28 | Plan 08 MS-M4 Build-tool shader resource registry report correlation | render_plan08_build_tool_resource_registry_backed_locator_correlation_python_passed_cargo_deferred | 审查发现：前一轮已把 `ResourceRecord.primary_locator` / `artifact_locator` wire shape 对齐 Rust `ResourceLocator::parse(...)`，但 report correlation 仍只收集 `res://` source labels；这会让 selected plugin 或 package shader 以 `lib://plugin/...`、`package://demo/...` 出现在 report 时，即使 auto-exported registry 漏记录也被当作成功。修复后 `_append_report_resource_source_label(...)` 改为通过 `_is_registry_backed_resource_locator_string(...)` 收集 `res/lib/package/mem`，并继续跳过 `builtin://` 内建 source；新增 `test_validate_registry_export_contract_accepts_registry_backed_source_locators` 与 `test_validate_registry_export_contract_rejects_missing_registry_backed_source_locator`。审查结论：registry/report locator coverage 漏洞已关闭在 build-helper owner 内；真实 staged WGPU prewarm、RenderDoc/product capture、full live registry export 和二次启动 miss=0 仍未关闭。 |

## 2026-06-28 Plan 08 resource registry Ready shader revision contract 审查补记

| 日期 | 范围 | 状态 | 审查发现处理记录 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-06-28 | Plan 08 MS-M4 Resource registry ready shader revision contract | render_plan08_resource_registry_ready_shader_revision_contract_python_static_passed_cargo_deferred | 审查发现：上一轮 usable shader revision contract 只要求 `kind=Shader` 且 `revision > 0`，但 live `ResourceManager::ready_records_for_kind(ResourceKind::Shader)` 还要求 `ResourceState::Ready`；这会让 `Error`/`Pending`/`Reloading` shader record 只要带非零 revision 就能满足 build-tool report correlation，Rust prewarm overlay 也可能把非 Ready revision 写入 variant key。修复后 `_is_usable_shader_record(...)` 要求 `state == "Ready"`，`ShaderPrewarmResourceRegistryOverlay::from_records(...)` 通过 `record.state != ResourceState::Ready` 跳过非 Ready rows；新增 `test_validate_registry_export_contract_rejects_non_ready_report_source_record` 与 `shader_prewarm_resource_registry_overlay_uses_ready_shader_revisions_only`。审查结论：Ready-state registry revision 漏洞已关闭在 build-helper 与 Rust overlay owner 内；真实 staged WGPU prewarm、full live project/plugin export、RenderDoc/product capture、fallback-root hit 和二次启动 miss=0 仍未关闭。 |

| 日期 | 范围 | 状态 | 审查发现处理记录 |

| 2026-06-28 | Plan 08 MS-M4 Build-tool shader prewarm report dimension complete-count contract | render_plan08_build_tool_report_dimension_complete_counts_python_passed_cargo_deferred | 审查发现：requested-dimension report contract 已能拒绝缺少 `forward/high/skinned/custom-id` 等维度的 report，但只看 `requested_count > 0`；如果 report 写成 `forward requested=6 written=5 failed=1`，build-helper 仍会把该 requested pass 当作维度覆盖成功，再等后续 cache/source gate 才可能暴露不一致。本轮在 report contract owner 内补 `_incomplete_dimension_counts(...)`，要求每个 expected dimension 同时满足 `written_count == requested_count` 且 `failed_count == 0`；新增 `test_validate_report_contract_rejects_incomplete_requested_dimension_counts`。审查结论：report dimension summary 的 incomplete-count 漏洞已关闭；真实 staged WGPU prewarm、RenderDoc/product capture、full live registry export 和二次启动 miss=0 仍未关闭。 |

| 2026-06-28 | Plan 08 MS-M4 Build-tool written variant uniqueness contract | render_plan08_build_tool_written_variant_uniqueness_contract_python_passed_cargo_deferred | 审查发现：前序 written variant identity acceptance 已要求字段完整并让 `written_count` 与 `written_variants` 数量匹配，但旧 helper 仍允许两条 report rows 使用同一个 `cache_hash` 或同一个 `canonical_string`；这会让重复 identity 伪装成两个写入结果，掩盖实际缺少的 product material variant。修复后 `tools/zircon_build_shader_prewarm_written_variants.py` 统一解析 written variants 并通过 `validate_unique_written_variant_identity(...)` 拒绝重复 `cache_hash` / `canonical_string`，cache artifact helper 和 acceptance helper 都复用该闸门；新增 `test_validate_cache_artifact_contract_rejects_duplicate_written_variant_identity` 与 `test_acceptance_contract_rejects_duplicate_written_variant_identity`。验证：focused cache+acceptance 36/36、build-helper 聚合 95/95、py_compile、rustfmt check、anchor/conflict/trailing-whitespace scans、line-budget scan 与 scoped diff-check 通过；diff-check 仅报告 LF/CRLF warning。审查结论：duplicate written cache variant identity 漏洞已关闭在 build-helper owner 内；真实 staged WGPU prewarm、RenderDoc/product capture、full live registry export 和二次启动 miss=0 仍未关闭。 |
